ZJournal\+::enqueue enqueues a new journaling event to be processed by journaling thread and written to journaling file. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
journaling thread is based on static routine ZJournal\+::process() 
\end{DoxyNote}

ZJournal\+::init initialize journaling process for the ZMF file (pointed by Journaling\+::\+Father). 


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Adequate parameters must have been set within Father\textquotesingle{}s ZMCB \+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Journaling\+On \+: must be set to true for journaling to start
\item Journal\+Path \+: pointing to a valid local directory or empty if omitted
\end{DoxyItemize}
\item Actions
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item opens journaling file.
\item initialize parameters of a thread in charge of processing the journaling event queue
\end{DoxyItemize}

Journal file must exist prior to execute this routine. see ZJournal\+::create\+File()

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{void}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} ZSJournal\+::process (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{zdataconversion_8h_abca6a6e5a79bb4856b5a33f58812c899}{void}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{p\+Journal }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}



ZJournal\+::process processes the journaling event queue. Infinite loop waiting for an enqueue operation. 


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item opens the journaling file (file path must have been set previously with \doxylink{classzbs_1_1_z_random_file_a097cbb67f4928d593dbb852f7b417ba0}{ZRandom\+File\+::set\+Path()} routine
\item infinite loop until receive ZJOP\+\_\+\+Close Operation code in event queue
\item waits for conditional mutex
\item to be waked up using signal or broadcast
\item dequeue event queue till empty
\item write each event into journal file
\item in the end \+: close journal file
\end{DoxyItemize}

\end{DoxyParams}
